Our findings expose inherent vulnerabilities in LiDAR-based SLAM and introduce the first practical defense against LiDAR-based SLAM spoofing using only standard onboard sensors, providing critical insights for improving the security and reliability of autonomous systems.

</details>


### [149] [Vision-Based Hand Shadowing for Robotic Manipulation via Inverse Kinematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11383)
*Hendrik Chiche,Antoine Jamme,Trevor Rigoberto Martinez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于单目RGB-D相机的离线手部影射与重定向管道，用于低成本机械臂遥操作，通过手部关键点检测、3D重建、坐标变换和逆运动学求解生成关节指令，在结构化任务中达到90%成功率，但在非结构化环境中因遮挡问题成功率降至9.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低成本机械臂遥操作面临的主要挑战是将复杂的人手关节运动映射到机器人关节指令。现有方法需要复杂的映射系统，而本文旨在开发一种基于单目RGB-D相机的简单、低成本解决方案。

Method: 使用安装在3D打印眼镜上的单目RGB-D相机采集数据；通过MediaPipe Hands检测每只手21个关键点；利用深度感知将2D关键点反投影到3D空间；将3D点变换到机器人坐标系；在PyBullet中使用阻尼最小二乘法求解逆运动学问题生成6自由度SO-ARM101机器人的关节指令；设计基于拇指-食指几何的四级回退层次抓取控制器；通过LeRobot框架在物理仿真中预览动作后在实际机器人上回放。

Result: 在结构化拾取放置基准测试（5瓦片网格，每瓦片10次抓取）中达到90%成功率；与四种基于视觉-语言-动作策略（ACT、SmolVLA、pi0.5、GR00T N1.5）相比表现良好；在非结构化真实环境（杂货店、药店）中，由于周围物体造成的手部遮挡，成功率降至9.3%（N=75）。

Conclusion: 基于标记的自由分析重定向方法在结构化任务中表现出色，但在非结构化环境中因遮挡问题效果有限，显示了该方法的潜力和当前局限性，为未来改进提供了方向。

Abstract: Teleoperation of low-cost robotic manipulators remains challenging due to the complexity of mapping human hand articulations to robot joint commands. We present an offline hand-shadowing and retargeting pipeline from a single egocentric RGB-D camera mounted on 3D-printed glasses. The pipeline detects 21 hand landmarks per hand using MediaPipe Hands, deprojects them into 3D via depth sensing, transforms them into the robot coordinate frame, and solves a damped-least-squares inverse kinematics problem in PyBullet to produce joint commands for the 6-DOF SO-ARM101 robot. A gripper controller maps thumb-index finger geometry to grasp aperture with a four-level fallback hierarchy. Actions are first previewed in a physics simulation before replay on the physical robot through the LeRobot framework. We evaluate the IK retargeting pipeline on a structured pick-and-place benchmark (5-tile grid, 10 grasps per tile) achieving a 90% success rate, and compare it against four vision-language-action policies (ACT, SmolVLA, pi0.5, GR00T N1.5) trained on leader-follower teleoperation data. We also test the IK pipeline in unstructured real-world environments (grocery store, pharmacy), where hand occlusion by surrounding objects reduces success to 9.3% (N=75), highlighting both the promise and current limitations of marker-free analytical retargeting.

</details>


### [150] [Deployment-Time Reliability of Learned Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11400)
*Christopher Agia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出三种部署时机制来提升学习型机器人策略的可靠性：运行时监控、基于影响函数的数据中心可解释性框架、以及面向长时程任务执行的策略协调方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管学习型机器人策略在能力上取得了显著进展，但部署时的可靠性仍然是实际应用的根本障碍。分布偏移、误差累积和复杂任务依赖关系共同削弱了系统性能，需要开发部署时机制来提升学习型策略的可靠性。

Method: 提出了三类互补的部署时机制：1) 运行时监控方法，通过识别闭环策略行为的不一致性和任务进展偏差来检测即将发生的故障，无需故障数据或任务特定监督；2) 基于影响函数的策略可解释性框架，将部署时的成功和失败追溯到有影响力的训练演示，实现原则性诊断和数据集管理；3) 可靠长时程任务执行方法，将策略协调形式化为估计和最大化行为序列成功概率的问题，并通过可行性感知的任务规划扩展到开放式语言指定任务。

Result: 这些部署时机制为中心的方法为学习型机器人策略的可靠实际使用提供了实用基础。通过运行时监控、数据驱动的可解释性和长时程任务协调，系统能够更好地应对分布偏移、误差累积和复杂任务依赖等挑战。

Conclusion: 通过专注于部署的核心挑战，这些贡献为学习型机器人策略的可靠实际应用推进了实用基础。在这些基础上的持续进步对于实现未来可信赖和可扩展的机器人自主性至关重要。

Abstract: Recent advances in learning-based robot manipulation have produced policies with remarkable capabilities. Yet, reliability at deployment remains a fundamental barrier to real-world use, where distribution shift, compounding errors, and complex task dependencies collectively undermine system performance. This dissertation investigates how the reliability of learned robot policies can be improved at deployment time through mechanisms that operate around them. We develop three complementary classes of deployment-time mechanisms. First, we introduce runtime monitoring methods that detect impending failures by identifying inconsistencies in closed-loop policy behavior and deviations in task progress, without requiring failure data or task-specific supervision. Second, we propose a data-centric framework for policy interpretability that traces deployment-time successes and failures to influential training demonstrations using influence functions, enabling principled diagnosis and dataset curation. Third, we address reliable long-horizon task execution by formulating policy coordination as the problem of estimating and maximizing the success probability of behavior sequences, and we extend this formulation to open-ended, language-specified tasks through feasibility-aware task planning. By centering on core challenges of deployment, these contributions advance practical foundations for the reliable, real-world use of learned robot policies. Continued progress on these foundations will be essential for enabling trustworthy and scalable robot autonomy in the future.

</details>


### [151] [Real-time Rendering-based Surgical Instrument Tracking via Evolutionary Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11404)
*Hanyang Hu,Zekai Liang,Florian Richter,Michael C. Yip*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于CMA-ES进化优化策略的手术器械跟踪框架，通过批量渲染并行评估多个位姿候选，实现高效准确的手术器械位姿和关节配置联合估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人辅助微创手术中，手术器械的准确高效跟踪至关重要。现有视觉方法在视觉质量下降和数据稀缺时特征检测不可靠，而渲染方法存在计算成本高和收敛性差的问题。手术器械的部分可见性和特殊关节设计使可靠跟踪更具挑战性。

Method: 将CMA-ES进化优化策略集成到多功能跟踪流程中，联合估计手术器械位姿和关节配置。采用批量渲染并行评估多个位姿候选，显著减少推理时间并提高收敛鲁棒性。框架可泛化到无关节角度和双手跟踪设置。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上的广泛实验表明，该方法在准确性和运行时间方面显著优于先前方法，适用于视觉反馈控制和在线手术视频校准。

Conclusion: 提出的基于CMA-ES的跟踪框架通过进化优化和批量渲染，实现了高效准确的手术器械跟踪，解决了现有方法在计算成本和收敛性方面的局限性，为机器人辅助手术提供了可靠的跟踪解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate and efficient tracking of surgical instruments is fundamental for Robot-Assisted Minimally Invasive Surgery. Although vision-based robot pose estimation has enabled markerless calibration without tedious physical setups, reliable tool tracking for surgical robots still remains challenging due to partial visibility and specialized articulation design of surgical instruments. Previous works in the field are usually prone to unreliable feature detections under degraded visual quality and data scarcity, whereas rendering-based methods often struggle with computational costs and suboptimal convergence. In this work, we incorporate CMA-ES, an evolutionary optimization strategy, into a versatile tracking pipeline that jointly estimates surgical instrument pose and joint configurations. Using batch rendering to efficiently evaluate multiple pose candidates in parallel, the method significantly reduces inference time and improves convergence robustness. The proposed framework further generalizes to joint angle-free and bi-manual tracking settings, making it suitable for both vision feedback control and online surgery video calibration. Extensive experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that the proposed method significantly outperforms prior approaches in both accuracy and runtime.

</details>


### [152] [Grounding Robot Generalization in Training Data via Retrieval-Augmented VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11426)
*Jensen Gao,Dorsa Sadigh,Sandy Huang,Dhruv Shah*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RADAR是一个评估机器人策略泛化能力的框架，通过检索训练数据和使用视觉语言模型分析测试任务，精确评估策略泛化类型


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操作研究在策略泛化评估方面缺乏精确性，难以量化测试场景与训练分布之间的差异，需要更精确的泛化评估方法

Method: 提出RADAR框架：1) 使用通用策略嵌入进行检索，识别与测试任务相关的训练示例；2) 使用视觉语言模型分析测试任务与检索数据的差异，输出可解释的分析和泛化类型分类

Result: 实验表明：1) VLM能有效分析泛化数据；2) 检索步骤能准确识别分类所需的相关训练示例；3) 在大规模数据集上，RADAR与人工定义的基准条件一致

Conclusion: RADAR提供了一个可扩展的框架，能够精确评估机器人策略的泛化能力，通过数据驱动的分析为泛化评估提供更精确的方法

Abstract: Recent work on robot manipulation has advanced policy generalization to novel scenarios. However, it is often difficult to characterize how different evaluation settings actually represent generalization from the training distribution of a given policy. To work towards more precise evaluation of generalization in robotics, we propose RADAR, a scalable framework for directly comparing test-time evaluation tasks to policy training data, to determine what form of policy generalization is required. RADAR consists of a two-stage pipeline: first, retrieval using generalist policy embeddings identifies which training examples are relevant for a given evaluation task. Next, vision-language models (VLMs) analyze the evaluation task against the retrieved data, outputting interpretable analysis on how they compare along a variety of axes, and an overall classification of what type of policy generalization is required. Through controlled experiments, we demonstrate that VLMs are effective at analyzing data for generalization, and that our retrieval step effectively identifies examples needed to make accurate classifications with respect to the training data. Furthermore, we scale RADAR to large-scale datasets, where we observe agreement with human-defined benchmark conditions from prior work. We provide demonstrations at radar-analysis.github.io.

</details>


### [153] [A Generalized Theory of Load Distribution in Redundantly-actuated Robotic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11431)
*Joshua Flight,Clément Gosselin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出冗余驱动多链机器人系统中刚性体载荷分布的广义理论，解决力合成与分析问题，提供高效计算方案并修正现有方法缺陷


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有冗余驱动机器人系统（如多指夹持器、腿式机器人、协作机器人）在刚性体载荷分布分析方面存在理论不足，缺乏高效的计算方法，需要建立统一的数学框架来解决力合成与分析的挑战

Method: 建立广义理论框架，完全描述刚性体在冗余驱动多链系统中的可行操纵力矩分布集；推导力矩合成与力矩分析问题的显式解；提出线性复杂度算法，避免数值方法和大型矩阵求逆

Result: 理论完全表征了给定合力矩下刚性体的可行力矩分布集；显式解计算效率高，复杂度与施加力矩数量呈线性关系；识别并修正了现有最先进方法的显著缺陷；通过示例验证改进方法的优势

Conclusion: 该理论为冗余驱动机器人系统的力控制提供了完整数学框架，解决了力矩分布、合成与分析的关键问题，提出的高效算法和修正方法对多指夹持器、腿式机器人、协作机器人等过约束机构具有重要应用价值

Abstract: This paper presents a generalized theory which describes how applied loads are distributed within rigid bodies handled by redundantly-actuated robotic systems composed of multiple independent closed-loop kinematic chains. The theory fully characterizes the feasible set of manipulating wrench distributions for a given resultant wrench applied to the rigid body and has important implications for the force-control of multifingered grippers, legged robots, cooperating robots, and other overconstrained mechanisms. We also derive explicit solutions to the wrench synthesis and wrench analysis problems. These solutions are computationally efficient and scale linearly with the number of applied wrenches, requiring neither numerical methods nor the inversion of large matrices. Finally, we identify significant shortcomings in current state-of-the-art approaches and propose corrections. These are supported by illustrative examples that demonstrate the advantages of the improved methods.

</details>


### [154] [Enhancing Lightweight Vision Language Models through Group Competitive Learning for Socially Compliant Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11447)
*Xinyu Zhang,Atsushi Konno,Toshihiko Yamasaki,Ling Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Group Competitive Learning (GCL)策略，通过Group Competitive Objective (GCO)和Asymmetric Group Optimization (AGO)增强轻量级视觉语言模型在社交导航中的推理能力，实现高精度与计算效率的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交机器人导航需要结合场景语义和人类社交规范。大型视觉语言模型(VLMs)推理能力强但计算开销大，不适合实时部署；轻量级VLMs效率高但推理能力弱。如何在保持高效率的同时获得强大推理能力是当前挑战。

Method: 提出Group Competitive Learning (GCL)策略，包含两个核心组件：1) Group Competitive Objective (GCO)：协调全局语义与分布正则化；2) Asymmetric Group Optimization (AGO)：探索模型性能上限。该方法旨在增强轻量级VLMs的推理能力。

Result: 在社交导航基准测试中，GCL显著提升了VLM性能：Qwen2.5-VL-3B模型F1分数达0.968（比普通监督微调提升40%），Qwen3-VL-4B达0.914（提升12%）。特别地，经过GCL训练的3B模型性能超过8B基线模型28%，证明了轻量模型通过该方法可实现超越更大模型的性能。

Conclusion: GCL为在现实世界部署中同时实现高精度和计算效率提供了有效解决方案，通过增强轻量级视觉语言模型的推理能力，解决了社交导航中模型大小与性能的权衡问题。

Abstract: Social robot navigation requires a sophisticated integration of scene semantics and human social norms. Scaling up Vision Language Models (VLMs) generally improves reasoning and decision-making capabilities for socially compliant navigation. However, increased model size incurs substantial computational overhead, limiting suitability for real-time robotic deployment. Conversely, lightweight VLMs enable efficient inference but often exhibit weaker reasoning and decision-making performance in socially complex environments. Achieving both strong reasoning ability and efficiency remains an open challenge. To bridge this gap, we propose Group Competitive Learning (GCL), a strategy designed to amplify the capabilities of lightweight VLMs. Our strategy introduces the Group Competitive Objective (GCO) to harmonize global semantics with distributional regularization, alongside Asymmetric Group Optimization (AGO) to explore the upper limits of model performance. Empirical evaluations on social navigation benchmarks demonstrate that GCL significantly elevates VLM performance. Specifically, GCL enables the Qwen2.5-VL-3B learner model and guide Qwen3-VL-4B to achieve an F1 score of 0.968 and 0.914, representing 40\% and 12\% improvement over vanilla supervised fine-tuning (SFT). Notably, under vanilla SFT, the 3B model initially trails the 8B model (F1: 0.692 vs. 0.755). However, through the GCL, the 3B model outperforms (28\%) the 8B baseline model. These results suggest that GCL provides an effective solution for achieving both high accuracy and computational efficiency in real-world deployment.

</details>


### [155] [SPARK: Skeleton-Parameter Aligned Retargeting on Humanoid Robots with Kinodynamic Trajectory Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11480)
*Hanwen Wang,Qiayuan Liao,Bike Zhang,Kunzhao Ren,Koushil Sreenath,Xiaobin Xiong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出两阶段流程：骨架校准+轨迹优化，将人类运动数据转化为机器人可用的动态可行运动参考


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类运动数据为训练人形机器人控制策略提供丰富先验，但原始演示与机器人运动学和动力学不兼容，限制了直接使用

Method: 两阶段流程：1) 骨架校准 - 将人类运动转换为URDF骨架表示并校准到目标人形机器人尺寸；2) 渐进式运动动力学轨迹优化 - 分三阶段：运动学TO、逆动力学、完整运动动力学TO，每阶段以前一阶段解为初始值

Result: 显著降低逆运动学误差和调参工作量，生成动态一致的状态轨迹和关节扭矩剖面，为基于学习的控制器提供高质量参考

Conclusion: 骨架校准和运动动力学轨迹优化相结合，能够为不同人形机器人平台生成自然、物理一致的运动参考

Abstract: Human motion provides rich priors for training general-purpose humanoid control policies, but raw demonstrations are often incompatible with a robot's kinematics and dynamics, limiting their direct use. We present a two-stage pipeline for generating natural and dynamically feasible motion references from task-space human data. First, we convert human motion into a unified robot description format (URDF)-based skeleton representation and calibrate it to the target humanoid's dimensions. By aligning the underlying skeleton structure rather than heuristically modifying task-space targets, this step significantly reduces inverse kinematics error and tuning effort. Second, we refine the retargeted trajectories through progressive kinodynamic trajectory optimization (TO), solved in three stages: kinematic TO, inverse dynamics, and full kinodynamic TO, each warm-started from the previous solution. The final result yields dynamically consistent state trajectories and joint torque profiles, providing high-quality references for learning-based controllers. Together, skeleton calibration and kinodynamic TO enable the generation of natural, physically consistent motion references across diverse humanoid platforms.

</details>


### [156] [MiNI-Q: A Miniature, Wire-Free Quadruped with Unbounded, Independently Actuated Leg Joints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11537)
*Daniel Koh,Suraj Shah,Yufeng Wu,Dennis Hong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MiNI-Q²是一款微型无线四足机器人，具有独立驱动的机械无限制2自由度腿部关节，可实现多种运动模式并支持开源设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统腿式机器人的物理关节限制会限制工作空间、约束步态设计并增加硬件损坏风险，需要开发具有无限制关节的机器人来克服这些限制。

Method: 提出MiNI-Q²机器人的机械设计、运动学分析和实验验证，采用独立驱动的机械无限制2自由度腿部关节，基于先前Q8bot设计改进无线架构以提高微型尺度的装配可靠性。

Result: 机器人可实现振荡步态和旋转运动，最小折叠高度2.5厘米，最高速度0.46米/秒，能够完成低间隙爬行、爬楼梯、倒立行走、跳跃和后空翻等多种运动，所有机械和电气设计文件开源发布。

Conclusion: MiNI-Q²展示了机械无限制关节在微型四足机器人中的可行性，为步态设计和运动能力提供了新的可能性，开源设计将促进可重复性和进一步研究。

Abstract: Physical joint limits are common in legged robots and can restrict workspace, constrain gait design, and increase the risk of hardware damage. This paper introduces MiNI-Q^2, a miniature, wire-free quadruped robot with independently actuated, mechanically unbounded 2-DOF leg joints. We present the mechanical design, kinematic analysis, and experimental validation of the proposed robot. The leg mechanism enables both oscillatory gaits and rotary locomotion while allowing the robot to fold to a minimum height of 2.5 cm. Experimentally, MiNI-Q achieves speeds up to 0.46 m/s and demonstrates low-clearance crawling, stair climbing, inverted locomotion, jumping, and backflipping. The wire-free architecture extends our previous Q8bot design, improving assembly reliability at miniature scale. All mechanical and electrical design files are released open source to support reproducibility and further research.

</details>


### [157] [RoboClaw: An Agentic Framework for Scalable Long-Horizon Robotic Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11558)
*Ruiying Li,Yunlang Zhou,YuYao Zhu,Kylin Chen,Jingyuan Wang,Sukai Wang,Kongtao Hu,Minhui Yu,Bowen Jiang,Zhan Su,Jiayao Ma,Xin He,Yongjian Shen,Yangyang,Guanghui Ren,Maoqing Yao,Wenhao Wang,Yao Mu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboClaw是一个统一的VLA机器人框架，通过自重置循环实现自主数据收集和策略学习，显著提升长时程任务的成功率并减少人工干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA系统在长时程任务中存在扩展性挑战，传统流水线将数据收集、策略学习和部署分离，导致严重依赖人工环境重置和脆弱的多策略执行。

Method: 提出RoboClaw框架，统一数据收集、策略学习和任务执行于单一VLM控制器下。引入纠缠动作对(EAP)，将前向操作行为与逆向恢复动作耦合，形成自重置循环，实现自主数据收集和迭代策略优化。

Result: 在真实世界操作任务中，相比传统开环流水线，RoboClaw提升了稳定性和可扩展性，长时程任务成功率提高25%，人工时间投入减少53.7%。

Conclusion: RoboClaw通过统一的VLA驱动控制器和自重置数据收集机制，有效解决了长时程任务中的扩展性问题，显著减少了机器人生命周期中的人工干预需求。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) systems have shown strong potential for language-driven robotic manipulation. However, scaling them to long-horizon tasks remains challenging. Existing pipelines typically separate data collection, policy learning, and deployment, resulting in heavy reliance on manual environment resets and brittle multi-policy execution. We present RoboClaw, an agentic robotics framework that unifies data collection, policy learning, and task execution under a single VLM-driven controller. At the policy level, RoboClaw introduces Entangled Action Pairs (EAP), which couple forward manipulation behaviors with inverse recovery actions to form self-resetting loops for autonomous data collection. This mechanism enables continuous on-policy data acquisition and iterative policy refinement with minimal human intervention. During deployment, the same agent performs high-level reasoning and dynamically orchestrates learned policy primitives to accomplish long-horizon tasks. By maintaining consistent contextual semantics across collection and execution, RoboClaw reduces mismatch between the two phases and improves multi-policy robustness. Experiments in real-world manipulation tasks demonstrate improved stability and scalability compared to conventional open-loop pipelines, while significantly reducing human effort throughout the robot lifecycle, achieving a 25% improvement in success rate over baseline methods on long-horizon tasks and reducing human time investment by 53.7%.

</details>


### [158] [Unsupervised LiDAR-Based Multi-UAV Detection and Tracking Under Extreme Sparsity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11586)
*Nivand Khosravi,Rodrigo Ventura,Meysam Basiri*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出无监督激光雷达检测与跟踪管道，解决固态激光雷达对空中无人机扫描稀疏性问题，通过自适应聚类和概率数据关联提升多目标跟踪性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固态激光雷达对空中无人机的非重复扫描导致测量极其稀疏（10-25米距离的小型四旋翼每扫描仅产生1-2个回波），远低于现有检测方法假设的点云密度，且不足以进行鲁棒的多目标数据关联

Method: 无监督激光雷达专用管道，包含检测和跟踪两部分。检测器采用距离自适应DBSCAN聚类结合三阶段时间一致性检查。多目标跟踪比较确定性匈牙利分配与联合概率数据关联（JPDA），均与交互多模型滤波结合。采用两环境评估策略：真实世界检测配RTK-GPS地面真值，仿真跟踪配身份标注地面真值

Result: 最佳检测配置达到0.891精度、0.804召回率和0.63米RMSE。minPts参数扫描验证大多数扫描最多包含1-2个目标点，直接量化稀疏性。在四个模拟场景中，JPDA将身份切换减少64%，对MOTA影响可忽略，证明当无人机轨迹接近时概率关联具有优势

Conclusion: 提出的无监督激光雷达管道有效解决了固态激光雷达对空中无人机检测的稀疏性问题，概率数据关联显著改善多目标跟踪性能，两环境评估策略克服了GNSS在2米以下间距的评估限制

Abstract: Non-repetitive solid-state LiDAR scanning leads to an extremely sparse measurement regime for detecting airborne UAVs: a small quadrotor at 10-25 m typically produces only 1-2 returns per scan, which is far below the point densities assumed by most existing detection approaches and inadequate for robust multi-target data association. We introduce an unsupervised, LiDAR-only pipeline that addresses both detection and tracking without the need for labeled training data. The detector integrates range-adaptive DBSCAN clustering with a three-stage temporal consistency check and is benchmarked on real-world air-to-air flight data under eight different parameter configurations. The best setup attains 0.891 precision, 0.804 recall, and 0.63 m RMSE, and a systematic minPts sweep verifies that most scans contain at most 1-2 target points, directly quantifying the sparsity regime. For multi-target tracking, we compare deterministic Hungarian assignment with joint probabilistic data association (JPDA), each coupled with Interacting Multiple Model filtering, in four simulated scenarios with increasing levels of ambiguity. JPDA cuts identity switches by 64% with negligible impact on MOTA, demonstrating that probabilistic association is advantageous when UAV trajectories approach one another closely. A two-environment evaluation strategy, combining real-world detection with RTK-GPS ground truth and simulation-based tracking with identity-annotated ground truth, overcomes the limitations of GNSS-only evaluation at inter-UAV distances below 2 m.

</details>


### [159] [Diversity You Can Actually Measure: A Fast, Model-Free Diversity Metric for Robotics Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11634)
*Sreevardhan Sirigiri,Nathan Samuel de Lara,Christopher Agia,Florian Shkurti,Fabio Ramos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出FAKTUAL算法，利用轨迹核熵进行机器人模仿学习的数据集多样性评估与筛选，无需模型访问即可提升下游任务成功率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人模仿学习数据集通常包含不同长度的长时程轨迹，难以在尊重轨迹结构和几何特性的前提下量化多样性。现有方法缺乏直接基于演示数据集的结构化多样性度量

Method: 扩展香农熵和冯·诺依曼熵到轨迹数据，基于签名核的Gram矩阵定义签名变换熵。提出FAKTUAL算法，通过最大化熵来选择给定预算下的最优演示子集，完全无需模型访问

Result: 在图像和状态基的RoboMimic、MetaWorld基准测试以及四个真实世界操作任务上，FAKTUAL相比随机选择持续提升下游成功率，计算效率显著高于现有机器人数据筛选方法

Conclusion: 演示数据集的熵是理解和改进机器人模仿学习中数据集多样性的实用工具，FAKTUAL提供了一种简单、模型无关的数据筛选方法

Abstract: Robotics datasets for imitation learning typically consist of long-horizon trajectories of different lengths over states, actions, and high-dimensional observations (e.g., RGB video), making it non-trivial to quantify diversity in a way that respects the underlying trajectory structure and geometry. We extend Shannon and von Neumann entropy to this setting by defining signature transform-based entropy on the Gram matrix of a signature kernel over demonstrations, yielding entropy and diversity metrics that operate directly on the demonstration dataset. Building on these metrics, we study how dataset diversity affects generalization performance in robot imitation learning and propose a simple, model-free way to curate diverse demonstrations. We introduce FAKTUAL (FAst trajectory Kernel enTropy cUration for imitation Learning), a data curation algorithm that selects a subset of demonstrations maximizing entropy given a subset-size budget. FAKTUAL is fully model-free, requires no access to the imitation policy or rollouts, and adds negligible overhead relative to policy training. We evaluate our approach on image and state-based RoboMimic and MetaWorld benchmarks, as well as four real-world manipulation tasks. Across tasks and architectures, diversity-aware curation with FAKTUAL consistently improves downstream success rates over random selection, while being substantially more computationally efficient compared to recent robot data curation methods. Our results suggest that the entropy of demonstration datasets is a practical tool for understanding and improving dataset diversity in robot imitation learning.

</details>


### [160] [Learn Structure, Adapt on the Fly: Multi-Scale Residual Learning and Online Adaptation for Aerial Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11638)
*Samaksh Ujjawal,Naveen Sudheer Nair,Shivansh Pratap Singh,Rishabh Dev Yadav,Wei Pan,Spandan Roy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Factorized Dynamics Transformer (FDT)和Latent Residual Adapter (LRA)的预测-自适应框架，用于自主空中机械臂的非平稳多尺度残差动力学实时建模与补偿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主空中机械臂是固有的耦合非线性系统，在机械臂重构和突加载荷变化时表现出非平稳和多尺度残差动力学。传统解析模型依赖固定参数结构，静态数据驱动模型假设平稳动力学且在配置变化和载荷变化时性能下降。现有学习架构未显式分解跨变量耦合和多尺度时间效应，混淆了瞬时惯性动力学与长期状态演化。

Method: 提出预测-自适应框架，核心是Factorized Dynamics Transformer (FDT)，将物理变量作为独立token处理，显式实现跨变量注意力，结构上分离短时域惯性依赖和长时域气动效应。为应对部署时分布偏移，Latent Residual Adapter (LRA)通过递归最小二乘法在潜空间进行快速线性自适应，保留离线非线性表示而不产生过高计算开销。自适应残差预测直接集成到残差补偿自适应控制器中。

Result: 在承受未知载荷的空中机械臂上进行真实世界实验，相比最先进的学习基线，展示了更高的预测保真度、更快的扰动衰减和更优的闭环跟踪精度，同时保持严格的实时可行性。

Conclusion: 提出的FDT-LRA框架成功解决了自主空中机械臂的非平稳多尺度残差动力学建模问题，通过显式变量分解和快速潜空间自适应，实现了高性能的实时残差补偿控制。

Abstract: Autonomous Aerial Manipulators (AAMs) are inherently coupled, nonlinear systems that exhibit nonstationary and multiscale residual dynamics, particularly during manipulator reconfiguration and abrupt payload variations. Conventional analytical dynamic models rely on fixed parametric structures, while static data-driven model assume stationary dynamics and degrade under configuration changes and payload variations. Moreover, existing learning architectures do not explicitly factorize cross-variable coupling and multi-scale temporal effects, conflating instantaneous inertial dynamics with long-horizon regime evolution. We propose a predictive-adaptive framework for real-time residual modeling and compensation in AAMs. The core of this framework is the Factorized Dynamics Transformer (FDT), which treats physical variables as independent tokens. This design enables explicit cross-variable attention while structurally separating short-horizon inertial dependencies from long-horizon aerodynamic effects. To address deployment-time distribution shifts, a Latent Residual Adapter (LRA) performs rapid linear adaptation in the latent space via Recursive Least Squares, preserving the offline nonlinear representation without prohibitive computational overhead. The adapted residual forecast is directly integrated into a residual-compensated adaptive controller. Real-world experiments on an aerial manipulator subjected to unseen payloads demonstrate higher prediction fidelity, accelerated disturbance attenuation, and superior closed-loop tracking precision compared to state-of-the-art learning baselines, all while maintaining strict real-time feasibility.

</details>


### [161] [Chunk-Boundary Artifact in Action-Chunked Generative Policies: A Noise-Sensitive Failure Mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11642)
*Rui Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究发现动作分块策略中的边界伪影是一种噪声敏感故障机制，可通过噪声空间干预调节


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动作分块已成为生成式视觉运动策略的核心设计选择，但分块边界处的执行不连续性机制仍不明确，需要系统分析边界伪影的成因和干预方法

Method: 在预训练的动作分块策略中，通过统计分析关联伪影与任务失败，在固定观测上下文下改变潜在噪声调节伪影幅度，识别噪声空间中的伪影相关方向并进行轨迹级干预

Result: 边界伪影与任务失败显著相关(p<1e-4)，噪声变化可系统调节伪影幅度，通过噪声空间干预可可靠改变所有评估任务的伪影幅度；在困难任务中成功/失败分布相应变化，在接近上限任务中正增益被策略饱和压缩但负因果效应仍可见

Conclusion: 将边界不连续性从不可避免的执行干扰重新定义为可分析、噪声主导且可干预的故障机制

Abstract: Action chunking has become a central design choice for generative visuomotor policies, yet the execution discontinuities that arise at chunk boundaries remain poorly understood. In a frozen pretrained action-chunked policy, we identify chunk-boundary artifact as a noise-sensitive failure mechanism. First, artifact is strongly associated with task failure (p < 1e-4, permutation test) and emerges during the rollout rather than only as a post-hoc symptom. Second, under a fixed observation context, changing only latent noise systematically modulates artifact magnitude. Third, by identifying artifact-related directions in noise space and applying trajectory-level steering, we reliably alter artifact magnitude across all evaluated tasks. In hard-task settings with remaining outcome headroom, the success/failure distribution shifts accordingly; on near-ceiling tasks, positive gains are compressed by policy saturation, while the negative causal effect remains visible. Overall, we recast boundary discontinuity from an unavoidable execution nuisance into an analyzable, noise-dominated, and intervenable failure mechanism.

</details>


### [162] [Concurrent Prehensile and Nonprehensile Manipulation: A Practical Approach to Multi-Stage Dexterous Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11655)
*Hao Jiang,Yue Wu,Yue Wang,Gaurav S. Sukhatme,Daniel Seita*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexMulti：一种样本高效的灵巧多任务操作方法，通过检索-对齐-执行范式分解演示为对象中心技能，在少量演示下实现多阶段操作任务


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧手能够同时进行抓握和非抓握操作（如持握一个物体同时与另一个物体交互），这是日常任务的关键能力但在机器人领域研究不足。学习这种长时程、接触丰富的多阶段行为具有挑战性，因为演示收集成本高，端到端策略需要大量数据才能泛化到不同的物体几何形状和放置位置。

Method: DexMulti将演示分解为具有明确时间边界的对象中心技能，而非学习单一策略。方法采用检索-对齐-执行范式：1) 基于当前物体几何检索演示技能；2) 使用不确定性感知估计器（跟踪质心和偏航角）将技能对齐到观察到的物体状态；3) 执行对齐后的技能。

Result: 在三个多阶段任务（抓握+拉动、抓握+打开、抓握+抓握）和两种灵巧手（Allegro和LEAP）上进行了1000多次真实世界试验。方法仅需每个物体3-4个演示，在训练物体上平均成功率66%，比扩散策略基线高2-3倍，同时需要更少演示。结果展示了对外推物体和±25厘米空间变化的鲁棒泛化能力。

Conclusion: DexMulti提供了一种样本高效的灵巧多任务操作方法，通过分解演示为对象中心技能并采用检索-对齐-执行范式，能够在少量演示下实现复杂多阶段操作任务，并具有良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Dexterous hands enable concurrent prehensile and nonprehensile manipulation, such as holding one object while interacting with another, a capability essential for everyday tasks yet underexplored in robotics. Learning such long-horizon, contact-rich multi-stage behaviors is challenging because demonstrations are expensive to collect and end-to-end policies require substantial data to generalize across varied object geometries and placements. We present DexMulti, a sample-efficient approach for real-world dexterous multi-task manipulation that decomposes demonstrations into object-centric skills with well-defined temporal boundaries. Rather than learning monolithic policies, our method retrieves demonstrated skills based on current object geometry, aligns them to the observed object state using an uncertainty-aware estimator that tracks centroid and yaw, and executes them via a retrieve-align-execute paradigm. We evaluate on three multi-stage tasks requiring concurrent manipulation (Grasp + Pull, Grasp + Open, and Grasp + Grasp) across two dexterous hands (Allegro and LEAP) in over 1,000 real-world trials. Our approach achieves an average success rate of 66% on training objects with only 3-4 demonstrations per object, outperforming diffusion policy baselines by 2-3x while requiring far fewer demonstrations. Results demonstrate robust generalization to held-out objects and spatial variations up to +/-25 cm.

</details>


### [163] [Coupling Tensor Trains with Graph of Convex Sets: Effective Compression, Exploration, and Planning in the C-Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11658)
*Gerhard Reinerth,Riddhiman Laha,Marcello Romano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TANGO框架结合张量压缩与图优化，实现高效可扩展的运动规划


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于优化的运动规划器（如GCS）需要预定义高维构型空间的凸表征，这在通用机器人任务中往往难以实现，限制了其应用范围

Method: 使用张量链分解近似可行构型空间的压缩表示，快速发现任务相关区域，并将其嵌入GCS类结构中，实现几何感知的运动规划

Result: 在平面和真实机器人上的仿真研究表明，TANGO实现了有效压缩并生成更高质量的轨迹

Conclusion: TANGO通过张量压缩与结构化图推理的结合，实现了高效几何感知的运动规划，为未来机器人系统中更表达性强、可扩展的构型空间表示奠定了基础

Abstract: We present TANGO (Tensor ANd Graph Optimization), a novel motion planning framework that integrates tensor-based compression with structured graph optimization to enable efficient and scalable trajectory generation. While optimization-based planners such as the Graph of Convex Sets (GCS) offer powerful tools for generating smooth, optimal trajectories, they typically rely on a predefined convex characterization of the high-dimensional configuration space-a requirement that is often intractable for general robotic tasks. TANGO builds further by using Tensor Train decomposition to approximate the feasible configuration space in a compressed form, enabling rapid discovery and estimation of task-relevant regions. These regions are then embedded into a GCS-like structure, allowing for geometry-aware motion planning that respects both system constraints and environmental complexity. By coupling tensor-based compression with structured graph reasoning, TANGO enables efficient, geometry-aware motion planning and lays the groundwork for more expressive and scalable representations of configuration space in future robotic systems. Rigorous simulation studies on planar and real robots reinforce our claims of effective compression and higher quality trajectories.

</details>


### [164] [RADAR: Closed-Loop Robotic Data Generation via Semantic Planning and Autonomous Causal Environment Reset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11811)
*Yongzhong Wang,Keyu Zhu,Yong Zhong,Liqiong Wang,Jinyu Yang,Feng Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RADAR是一个完全自主的机器人数据采集系统，通过四模块流水线消除人工干预，实现大规模物理交互数据的自动化收集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器人学习所需的大规模物理交互数据采集受到人工参与成本高和可扩展性限制的严重瓶颈，需要打破这一障碍。

Method: RADAR采用四模块流水线：1) VLM基于2-5个3D人体演示生成场景相关任务；2) GNN策略通过上下文模仿学习将子任务转化为物理动作；3) VLM通过结构化视觉问答进行自动成功评估；4) 有限状态机协调自主环境重置和不对称数据路由机制。

Result: 在仿真中，RADAR在复杂长时程任务上达到90%成功率，远超传统基线方法；在真实世界部署中，系统通过少样本适应可靠执行多样化接触密集型技能，无需领域特定微调。

Conclusion: RADAR提供了一个高度可扩展的机器人数据采集范式，将数据收集转变为自维持过程，解决了大规模物理交互数据获取的关键瓶颈问题。

Abstract: The acquisition of large-scale physical interaction data, a critical prerequisite for modern robot learning, is severely bottlenecked by the prohibitive cost and scalability limits of human-in-the-loop collection paradigms. To break this barrier, we introduce Robust Autonomous Data Acquisition for Robotics (RADAR), a fully autonomous, closed-loop data generation engine that completely removes human intervention from the collection cycle. RADAR elegantly divides the cognitive load into a four-module pipeline. Anchored by 2-5 3D human demonstrations as geometric priors, a Vision-Language Model first orchestrates scene-relevant task generation via precise semantic object grounding and skill retrieval. Next, a Graph Neural Network policy translates these subtasks into physical actions via in-context imitation learning. Following execution, the VLM performs automated success evaluation using a structured Visual Question Answering pipeline. Finally, to shatter the bottleneck of manual resets, a Finite State Machine orchestrates an autonomous environment reset and asymmetric data routing mechanism. Driven by simultaneous forward-reverse planning with a strict Last-In, First-Out causal sequence, the system seamlessly restores unstructured workspaces and robustly recovers from execution failures. This continuous brain-cerebellum synergy transforms data collection into a self-sustaining process. Extensive evaluations highlight RADAR's exceptional versatility. In simulation, our framework achieves up to 90% success rates on complex, long-horizon tasks, effortlessly solving challenges where traditional baselines plummet to near-zero performance. In real-world deployments, the system reliably executes diverse, contact-rich skills (e.g., deformable object manipulation) via few-shot adaptation without domain-specific fine-tuning, providing a highly scalable paradigm for robotic data acquisition.

</details>


### [165] [Energy Prediction on Sloping Ground for Quadruped Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11963)
*Mohamed Ounally,Cyrille Pierre,Johann Laconte*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于标准车载传感器的四足机器人能量模型，研究地形坡度和航向对能耗的影响，支持路径规划的能量评估


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足机器人在户外环境中的能量管理是核心挑战，续航直接影响任务成功率，而高效资源利用减少生态影响。需要研究地形坡度和航向如何影响四足运动能耗

Method: 引入简单能量模型，仅依赖标准车载传感器，避免专用仪器，适用于未探索环境。通过商业四足机器人的野外运行数据识别模型，表达为坡度和航向的紧凑函数

Result: 自然地形野外验证显示：力等效成本与坡度角呈近似线性趋势，横向成本始终更高，轨迹段具有可加性，支持路径级能量预测用于规划导向评估

Conclusion: 提出的能量模型能够准确预测四足机器人在不同地形条件下的能耗，为户外环境中的路径规划和能量管理提供了实用工具

Abstract: Energy management is a fundamental challenge for legged robots in outdoor environments. Endurance directly constrains mission success, while efficient resource use reduces ecological impact. This paper investigates how terrain slope and heading orientation influence the energetic cost of quadruped locomotion. We introduce a simple energy model that relies solely on standard onboard sensors, avoids specialized instrumentation, and remains applicable in previously unexplored environments. The model is identified from field runs on a commercial quadruped and expressed as a compact function of slope angle and heading. Field validation on natural terrain shows near-linear trends of force-equivalent cost with slope angle, consistently higher lateral costs, and additive behavior across trajectory segments, supporting path-level energy prediction for planning-oriented evaluation.

</details>


### [166] [Learning Visuomotor Policy for Multi-Robot Laser Tag Game](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11980)
*Kai Li,Shiyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种用于多机器人激光标签任务的端到端视觉运动策略，通过教师-学生知识蒸馏方法，将多智能体强化学习训练的教师策略转化为基于视觉的学生策略，在命中精度和碰撞避免方面优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统模块化方法在多机器人激光标签任务中面临有限观测性、依赖深度映射和机器人间通信等挑战，需要更有效的解决方案。

Method: 采用端到端视觉运动策略，将图像直接映射到机器人动作。首先使用多智能体强化学习训练高性能教师策略，然后通过知识蒸馏将其转化为基于视觉的学生策略。技术设计包括置换不变特征提取器和深度热图输入。

Result: 该方法在命中精度上比传统方法提高16.7%，在碰撞避免方面提高6%，并成功在真实机器人上部署。

Conclusion: 提出的端到端视觉运动策略有效解决了多机器人激光标签任务中的挑战，通过教师-学生知识蒸馏方法实现了高性能的视觉控制，在实际部署中表现出色。

Abstract: In this paper, we study multi robot laser tag, a simplified yet practical shooting-game-style task. Classic modular approaches on these tasks face challenges such as limited observability and reliance on depth mapping and inter robot communication. To overcome these issues, we present an end-to-end visuomotor policy that maps images directly to robot actions. We train a high performing teacher policy with multi agent reinforcement learning and distill its knowledge into a vision-based student policy. Technical designs, including a permutation-invariant feature extractor and depth heatmap input, improve performance over standard architectures. Our policy outperforms classic methods by 16.7% in hitting accuracy and 6% in collision avoidance, and is successfully deployed on real robots. Code will be released publicly.

</details>


### [167] [Sim-to-reality adaptation for Deep Reinforcement Learning applied to an underwater docking application](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12020)
*Alaaeddine Chaarani,Narcis Palomeras,Pere Ridao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于深度强化学习的自主水下对接方法，使用Girona AUV和高保真数字孪生环境，通过多进程RL框架加速训练，实现了超过90%的成功率并成功验证了从仿真到现实的迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习为自主水下对接提供了比传统控制方法更强的适应性，但"仿真到现实"的差距和高训练延迟仍然是实际部署的主要瓶颈。需要开发一种能够加速训练并确保现实世界性能的系统性方法。

Method: 1) 将Stonefish模拟器改造为多进程RL框架以加速学习过程；2) 集成真实的AUV动力学、碰撞模型和传感器噪声；3) 使用PPO算法开发6自由度控制策略；4) 在无头环境中训练，采用随机起始位置以确保泛化性能；5) 设计综合考虑距离、方向、动作平滑度和自适应碰撞惩罚的奖励函数以促进软对接。

Result: 1) 在仿真中智能体实现了超过90%的成功率；2) 在物理测试池中成功验证了仿真到现实的适应性；3) DRL控制器表现出涌现行为，如基于俯仰的制动和偏航振荡以辅助机械对齐。

Conclusion: 该方法通过高保真数字孪生环境和多进程RL框架有效解决了仿真到现实的差距和训练延迟问题，证明了深度强化学习在自主水下对接中的实用性和有效性，控制器表现出的涌现行为进一步增强了系统的适应性。

Abstract: Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) offers a robust alternative to traditional control methods for autonomous underwater docking, particularly in adapting to unpredictable environmental conditions. However, bridging the "sim-to-real" gap and managing high training latencies remain significant bottlenecks for practical deployment. This paper presents a systematic approach for autonomous docking using the Girona Autonomous Underwater Vehicle (AUV) by leveraging a high-fidelity digital twin environment. We adapted the Stonefish simulator into a multiprocessing RL framework to significantly accelerate the learning process while incorporating realistic AUV dynamics, collision models, and sensor noise. Using the Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) algorithm, we developed a 6-DoF control policy trained in a headless environment with randomized starting positions to ensure generalized performance. Our reward structure accounts for distance, orientation, action smoothness, and adaptive collision penalties to facilitate soft docking. Experimental results demonstrate that the agent achieved a success rate of over 90% in simulation. Furthermore, successful validation in a physical test tank confirmed the efficacy of the sim-to-reality adaptation, with the DRL controller exhibiting emergent behaviors such as pitch-based braking and yaw oscillations to assist in mechanical alignment.

</details>


### [168] [Flight through Narrow Gaps with Morphing-Wing Drones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12059)
*Julius Wanner,Hoang-Vu Phan,Charbel Toumieh,Dario Floreano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究人员开发了一种可变翼展无人机，通过飞行中折叠机翼来穿越小于翼展宽度的狭窄缝隙，并建立了包含非线性模型预测控制的控制框架来应对折叠机翼时的升力损失挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定翼无人机穿越狭窄缝隙的能力受限于其翼展宽度，这限制了其在复杂环境（如废墟、森林或城市建筑）中的自主飞行能力。受鸟类飞行中折叠翅膀穿越狭窄空间的启发，需要开发能够临时折叠机翼的无人机来突破这一限制。

Method: 1. 开发了适用于低飞行速度和大迎角状态的通用翼展折叠无人机气动模型；2. 将纵向无人机动力学集成到最优参考轨迹生成框架中；3. 采用具有运行时自适应成本和约束的非线性模型预测控制；4. 在130克翼展折叠无人机上进行实验验证。

Result: 在5-7 m/s的前飞速度下，穿越狭窄缝隙时的平均高度误差为5厘米；能够强制无人机在接近缝隙时完全折叠机翼，适应不同的阈值距离；轨迹分析显示无人机可以通过在缝隙前加速和上仰来补偿穿越缝隙时的升力损失；在硬件上实现了精确的缝隙穿越，同时保持恒定的前飞速度参考和接近恒定的高度。

Conclusion: 通过翼展折叠机制和先进的控制策略，无人机能够可靠地穿越小于其翼展宽度的狭窄缝隙，这为无人机在复杂受限环境中的自主飞行开辟了新途径，特别是在搜索救援、基础设施检查等应用中具有重要价值。

Abstract: The size of a narrow gap traversable by a fixed-wing drone is limited by its wingspan. Inspired by birds, here, we enable the traversal of a gap of sub-wingspan width and height using a morphing-wing drone capable of temporarily sweeping in its wings mid-flight. This maneuver poses control challenges due to sudden lift loss during gap-passage at low flight speeds and the need for precisely timed wing-sweep actuation ahead of the gap. To address these challenges, we first develop an aerodynamic model for general wing-sweep morphing drone flight including low flight speeds and post-stall angles of attack. We integrate longitudinal drone dynamics into an optimal reference trajectory generation and Nonlinear Model Predictive Control framework with runtime adaptive costs and constraints. Validated on a 130 g wing-sweep-morphing drone, our method achieves an average altitude error of 5 cm during narrow-gap passage at forward speeds between 5 and 7 m/s, whilst enforcing fully swept wings near the gap across variable threshold distances. Trajectory analysis shows that the drone can compensate for lift loss during gap-passage by accelerating and pitching upwards ahead of the gap to an extent that differs between reference trajectory optimization objectives. We show that our strategy also allows for accurate gap passage on hardware whilst maintaining a constant forward flight speed reference and near-constant altitude.

</details>


### [169] [Decentralized Cooperative Localization for Multi-Robot Systems with Asynchronous Sensor Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12075)
*Nivand Khosravi,Niusha Khosravi,Mohammad Bozorg,Masoud S. Bahraini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种去中心化协同定位框架，用于GPS拒止环境下非完整移动机器人，通过扩展卡尔曼滤波、异步传感器处理、自动坐标系对齐和双路标评估实现鲁棒定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决GPS拒止环境下非完整移动机器人的定位问题，特别是在通信基础设施有限、特征稀疏的封闭空间（如地下室、水下环境）中，传统定位方法效果不佳。

Method: 采用去中心化协同定位框架：每个机器人本地使用扩展卡尔曼滤波进行定位；仅在通信可用时共享测量信息；处理异步传感器数据；通过变换矩阵实现自动坐标系对齐；引入双路标评估框架（静态环境特征+移动机器人作为动态路标）。

Result: 在Gazebo仿真和真实地下室环境中，DCL优于集中式协同定位，RMSE减少34%；双路标变体进一步改善56%。框架在特征稀疏环境、急转弯等挑战性场景中表现鲁棒。

Conclusion: 提出的去中心化协同定位框架适用于封闭空间、水下环境和特征稀疏地形等挑战性领域，相比传统方法具有显著优势，通过信息共享和双路标策略提高了定位精度和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Decentralized cooperative localization (DCL) is a promising approach for nonholonomic mobile robots operating in GPS-denied environments with limited communication infrastructure. This paper presents a DCL framework in which each robot performs localization locally using an Extended Kalman Filter, while sharing measurement information during update stages only when communication links are available and companion robots are successfully detected by LiDAR. The framework preserves cross-correlation consistency among robot state estimates while handling asynchronous sensor data with heterogeneous sampling rates and accommodating accelerations during dynamic maneuvers. Unlike methods that require pre-aligned coordinate systems, the proposed approach allows robots to initialize with arbitrary reference-frame orientations and achieves automatic alignment through transformation matrices in both the prediction and update stages. To improve robustness in feature-sparse environments, we introduce a dual-landmark evaluation framework that exploits both static environmental features and mobile robots as dynamic landmarks. The proposed framework enables reliable detection and feature extraction during sharp turns, while prediction accuracy is improved through information sharing from mutual observations. Experimental results in both Gazebo simulation and real-world basement environments show that DCL outperforms centralized cooperative localization (CCL), achieving a 34% reduction in RMSE, while the dual-landmark variant yields an improvement of 56%. These results demonstrate the applicability of DCL to challenging domains such as enclosed spaces, underwater environments, and feature-sparse terrains where conventional localization methods are ineffective.

</details>


### [170] [CRAFT: A Tendon-Driven Hand with Hybrid Hard-Soft Compliance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12120)
*Leo Lin,Shivansh Patel,Jay Moon,Svetlana Lazebnik,Unnat Jain*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CRAFT手是一种肌腱驱动的拟人化手，采用混合刚柔顺应性设计，通过关节处软材料和连杆刚性结构实现接触丰富的操作，成本低于600美元并开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机械手在接触丰富的操作任务中存在局限性，需要一种能够处理冲击和负载分布不均的设计，同时保持紧凑轻量和高性价比。

Method: 采用混合刚柔顺应性设计：关节处使用软材料吸收冲击，连杆保持刚性承载负载；使用滚动接触关节表面确保重复运动路径；15个电机通过肌腱驱动手指，保持紧凑轻量；成本控制在600美元以下并开源。

Result: 结构测试显示强度和耐久性提升，同时保持可比重复性；遥操作中能更好处理易碎和低摩擦物品；覆盖Feix分类法中33/33种抓握；成本低于600美元。

Conclusion: CRAFT手通过创新的混合刚柔顺应性设计，在保持紧凑轻量的同时提高了机械手的操作性能和耐久性，其低成本开源特性有望促进机器人操作研究的发展。

Abstract: We introduce CRAFT hand, a tendon-driven anthropomorphic hand with hybrid hard-soft compliance for contact-rich manipulation. The design is based on a simple idea: contact is not uniform across the hand. Impacts concentrate at joints, while links carry most of the load. CRAFT places soft material at joints and keeps links rigid, and uses rollingcontact joint surfaces to keep flexion on repeatable motion paths. Fifteen motors mounted on the fingers drive the hand through tendons, keeping the form factor compact and the fingers light. In structural tests, CRAFT improves strength and endurance while maintaining comparable repeatability. In teleoperation, CRAFT improves handling of fragile and low-friction items, and the hand covers 33/33 grasps in the Feix taxonomy. The full design costs under $600 and will be released open-source with visionbased teleoperation and simulation integration. Project page: http://craft-hand.github.io/

</details>


### [171] [ComFree-Sim: A GPU-Parallelized Analytical Contact Physics Engine for Scalable Contact-Rich Robotics Simulation and Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12185)
*Chetan Borse,Zhixian Xie,Wei-Cheng Huang,Wanxin Jin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ComFree-Sim：基于无互补性接触建模的GPU并行物理引擎，通过闭式阻抗更新计算接触冲量，实现接触对解耦和GPU友好计算，在密集接触场景中实现近线性扩展和2-3倍吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统物理仿真在接触密集型机器人应用中受限于接触解析效率，主流引擎通过互补性约束或约束优化强制执行非穿透和库仑摩擦，需要每步迭代求解，计算成本随接触密度超线性增长。

Method: 提出无互补性接触建模的GPU并行分析接触物理引擎，通过库仑摩擦对偶锥中的阻抗式预测-校正更新闭式计算接触冲量，接触计算在接触对间解耦并在锥面可分离，自然映射到GPU内核。扩展为统一6D接触模型捕捉切向、扭转和滚动摩擦，引入实用对偶锥阻抗启发式方法。

Result: 与MuJoCo Warp相比，在密集接触场景中展示近线性运行时扩展和2-3倍吞吐量提升，物理保真度相当。在真实多指LEAP手的实时MPC和动态感知运动重定向中部署，低延迟仿真提高闭环成功率并实现接触密集型任务的高频控制。

Conclusion: ComFree-Sim通过无互补性接触建模和GPU并行化，解决了接触密集型机器人仿真的计算瓶颈，实现高效、可扩展的物理仿真，为实时控制应用提供实用解决方案。

Abstract: Physics simulation for contact-rich robotics is often bottlenecked by contact resolution: mainstream engines enforce non-penetration and Coulomb friction via complementarity constraints or constrained optimization, requiring per-step iterative solves whose cost grows superlinearly with contact density. We present ComFree-Sim, a GPU-parallelized analytical contact physics engine built on complementarity-free contact modeling. ComFree-Sim computes contact impulses in closed form via an impedance-style prediction--correction update in the dual cone of Coulomb friction. Contact computation decouples across contact pairs and becomes separable across cone facets, mapping naturally to GPU kernels and yielding near-linear runtime scaling with the number of contacts. We further extend the formulation to a unified 6D contact model capturing tangential, torsional, and rolling friction, and introduce a practical dual-cone impedance heuristic. ComFree-Sim is implemented in Warp and exposed through a MuJoCo-compatible interface as a drop-in backend alternative to MuJoCo Warp (MJWarp). Experiments benchmark penetration, friction behaviors, stability, and simulation runtime scaling against MJWarp, demonstrating near-linear scaling and 2--3 times higher throughput in dense contact scenes with comparable physical fidelity. We deploy ComFree-Sim in real-time MPC for in-hand dexterous manipulation on a real-world multi-fingered LEAP hand and in dynamics-aware motion retargeting, demonstrating that low-latency simulation yields higher closed-loop success rates and enables practical high-frequency control in contact-rich tasks.

</details>


### [172] [SaPaVe: Towards Active Perception and Manipulation in Vision-Language-Action Models for Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12193)
*Mengzhen Liu,Enshen Zhou,Cheng Chi,Yi Han,Shanyu Rong,Liming Chen,Pengwei Wang,Zhongyuan Wang,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SaPaVe是一个端到端框架，通过解耦相机控制与操作动作、采用自底向上训练策略，实现了语义驱动的主动感知与鲁棒操作的统一，在真实世界任务中比现有方法成功率提升31.25%


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以统一语义驱动的主动感知与鲁棒、视角不变的操作执行，需要一种数据高效且能协调感知与执行的端到端框架

Method: 提出SaPaVe框架：1) 解耦相机控制与操作动作而非共享动作空间；2) 自底向上训练策略：先在大规模数据集上训练语义相机控制，再用混合数据联合优化两种动作类型；3) 引入3D几何感知模块提升动态视角下的执行鲁棒性；4) 创建ActiveViewPose-200K数据集和ActiveManip-Bench基准

Result: 在仿真和真实环境中的大量实验表明，SaPaVe优于GR00T N1和π_0等最新视觉-语言-动作模型，在真实世界任务中成功率提升高达31.25%

Conclusion: 通过解耦但协调的策略训练的紧密耦合感知与执行，能够实现高效且可泛化的主动操作，证明了该方法在复杂场景机器人交互中的有效性

Abstract: Active perception and manipulation are crucial for robots to interact with complex scenes. Existing methods struggle to unify semantic-driven active perception with robust, viewpoint-invariant execution. We propose SaPaVe, an end-to-end framework that jointly learns these capabilities in a data-efficient manner. Our approach decouples camera and manipulation actions rather than placing them in a shared action space, and follows a bottom-up training strategy: we first train semantic camera control on a large-scale dataset, then jointly optimize both action types using hybrid data. To support this framework, we introduce ActiveViewPose-200K, a dataset of 200k image-language-camera movement pairs for semantic camera movement learning, and a 3D geometry-aware module that improves execution robustness under dynamic viewpoints. We also present ActiveManip-Bench, the first benchmark for evaluating active manipulation beyond fixed-view settings. Extensive experiments in both simulation and real-world environments show that SaPaVe outperforms recent vision-language-action models such as GR00T N1 and \(π_0\), achieving up to 31.25\% higher success rates in real-world tasks. These results show that tightly coupled perception and execution, when trained with decoupled yet coordinated strategies, enable efficient and generalizable active manipulation. Project page: https://lmzpai.github.io/SaPaVe

</details>


### [173] [HandelBot: Real-World Piano Playing via Fast Adaptation of Dexterous Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12243)
*Amber Xie,Haozhi Qi,Dorsa Sadigh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HandelBot框架通过两阶段流程实现精确双手钢琴演奏：首先通过结构化精炼修正空间对齐，然后通过残差强化学习学习精细校正动作，仅需30分钟物理交互数据即可显著超越直接仿真部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多指灵巧操作是机器人学长期挑战，高质量数据收集困难是主要瓶颈。现有仿真到现实迁移方法在毫米级精度任务（如双手钢琴演奏）中常失败，需要新方法解决精确操作问题。

Method: 采用两阶段框架：1) 结构化精炼阶段：基于仿真训练策略，通过物理试运行调整侧向手指关节修正空间对齐；2) 残差强化学习阶段：自主学习精细校正动作。仅需30分钟物理交互数据。

Result: 在五首公认歌曲的硬件实验中，HandelBot成功实现精确双手钢琴演奏。性能比直接仿真部署提升1.8倍，仅需30分钟物理交互数据。

Conclusion: HandelBot框架通过结合仿真策略和快速适应，有效解决了毫米级精度灵巧操作问题，为高精度机器人任务提供了数据高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Mastering dexterous manipulation with multi-fingered hands has been a grand challenge in robotics for decades. Despite its potential, the difficulty of collecting high-quality data remains a primary bottleneck for high-precision tasks. While reinforcement learning and simulation-to-real-world transfer offer a promising alternative, the transferred policies often fail for tasks demanding millimeter-scale precision, such as bimanual piano playing. In this work, we introduce HandelBot, a framework that combines a simulation policy and rapid adaptation through a two-stage pipeline. Starting from a simulation-trained policy, we first apply a structured refinement stage to correct spatial alignments by adjusting lateral finger joints based on physical rollouts. Next, we use residual reinforcement learning to autonomously learn fine-grained corrective actions. Through extensive hardware experiments across five recognized songs, we demonstrate that HandelBot can successfully perform precise bimanual piano playing. Our system outperforms direct simulation deployment by a factor of 1.8x and requires only 30 minutes of physical interaction data.

</details>


### [174] [HumDex:Humanoid Dexterous Manipulation Made Easy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12260)
*Liang Heng,Yihe Tang,Jiajun Xu,Henghui Bao,Di Huang,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HumDex是一个用于人形机器人全身灵巧操作的便携式遥操作系统，通过IMU运动追踪和学习式重定向方法解决现有系统的可移植性、遮挡和精度问题，并提出了两阶段模仿学习框架来高效收集高质量演示数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人全身灵巧操作中，高质量演示数据的高效收集是一个核心瓶颈。现有的遥操作系统存在可移植性有限、遮挡问题或精度不足等缺陷，阻碍了其在复杂全身任务中的应用。

Method: 1. 引入HumDex便携式遥操作系统，利用IMU运动追踪解决可移植性与精度的权衡；2. 提出基于学习的重定向方法，无需手动参数调整即可生成平滑自然的手部动作；3. 提出两阶段模仿学习框架：先在多样化人类运动数据上进行预训练学习可泛化的先验知识，然后在机器人数据上进行微调以弥合本体差异实现精确执行。

Result: HumDex系统能够实现准确的全身体追踪并保持易于部署。该方法显著提高了对新配置、新物体和新背景的泛化能力，同时最小化了数据采集成本。整个系统完全可复现并已开源。

Conclusion: HumDex系统解决了人形机器人全身灵巧操作中高质量演示数据收集的瓶颈问题，通过创新的遥操作技术和两阶段学习框架，为复杂全身任务提供了高效、精确且可泛化的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper investigates humanoid whole-body dexterous manipulation, where the efficient collection of high-quality demonstration data remains a central bottleneck.
  Existing teleoperation systems often suffer from limited portability, occlusion, or insufficient precision, which hinders their applicability to complex whole-body tasks. To address these challenges, we introduce HumDex, a portable teleoperation system designed for humanoid whole-body dexterous manipulation. Our system leverages IMU-based motion tracking to address the portability-precision trade-off, enabling accurate full-body tracking while remaining easy to deploy. For dexterous hand control, we further introduce a learning-based retargeting method that generates smooth and natural hand motions without manual parameter tuning. Beyond teleoperation, HumDex enables efficient collection of human motion data. Building on this capability, we propose a two-stage imitation learning framework that first pre-trains on diverse human motion data to learn generalizable priors, and then fine-tunes on robot data to bridge the embodiment gap for precise execution. We demonstrate that this approach significantly improves generalization to new configurations, objects, and backgrounds with minimal data acquisition costs. The entire system is fully reproducible and open-sourced at https://github.com/physical-superintelligence-lab/HumDex.

</details>


### [175] [$Ψ_0$: An Open Foundation Model Towards Universal Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12263)
*Songlin Wei,Hongyi Jing,Boqian Li,Zhenyu Zhao,Jiageng Mao,Zhenhao Ni,Sicheng He,Jie Liu,Xiawei Liu,Kaidi Kang,Sheng Zang,Weiduo Yuan,Marco Pavone,Di Huang,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Ψ₀是一个用于人形机器人定位操作任务的开源基础模型，通过解耦学习过程最大化异构数据源效用，使用两阶段训练范式：先在人类第一人称视频上预训练VLM骨干，再在人形机器人数据上后训练动作专家，仅需800小时人类视频和30小时机器人数据即超越基线40%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常通过在大规模多样化人类和人形机器人数据上联合训练来解决人形机器人定位操作问题，但由于人类与人形机器人在运动学和运动模式上的根本差异，这种策略效果不佳。尽管数据量庞大，但数据效率和模型性能仍不理想。

Method: 提出解耦学习过程的两阶段训练范式：1）在人类第一人称视频上自回归预训练视觉语言模型骨干，获得可泛化的视觉-动作表示；2）在高质量人形机器人数据上后训练基于流的动作专家，学习精确的机器人关节控制。强调数据配方的重要性：使用高质量人类操作数据进行预训练，再在特定领域真实世界人形机器人轨迹上进行后训练。

Result: Ψ₀仅需约800小时人类视频数据和30小时真实世界机器人数据，在多个任务中的总体成功率比使用超过10倍数据预训练的基线高出40%以上。模型在真实世界实验中表现出最佳性能。

Conclusion: 通过解耦学习过程并采用优化的数据配方，Ψ₀能够高效利用异构数据源，显著提升人形机器人定位操作任务的性能。该方法证明了高质量领域特定数据的重要性，而非单纯依赖数据规模。团队将开源整个生态系统，包括数据处理训练流程、人形基础模型和实时动作推理引擎。

Abstract: We introduce $Ψ_0$ (Psi-Zero), an open foundation model to address challenging humanoid loco-manipulation tasks. While existing approaches often attempt to address this fundamental problem by co-training on large and diverse human and humanoid data, we argue that this strategy is suboptimal due to the fundamental kinematic and motion disparities between humans and humanoid robots. Therefore, data efficiency and model performance remain unsatisfactory despite the considerable data volume. To address this challenge, \ours\;decouples the learning process to maximize the utility of heterogeneous data sources. Specifically, we propose a staged training paradigm with different learning objectives: First, we autoregressively pre-train a VLM backbone on large-scale egocentric human videos to acquire generalizable visual-action representations. Then, we post-train a flow-based action expert on high-quality humanoid robot data to learn precise robot joint control. Our research further identifies a critical yet often overlooked data recipe: in contrast to approaches that scale with noisy Internet clips or heterogeneous cross-embodiment robot datasets, we demonstrate that pre-training on high-quality egocentric human manipulation data followed by post-training on domain-specific real-world humanoid trajectories yields superior performance. Extensive real-world experiments demonstrate that \ours\ achieves the best performance using only about 800 hours of human video data and 30 hours of real-world robot data, outperforming baselines pre-trained on more than 10$\times$ as much data by over 40\% in overall success rate across multiple tasks. We will open-source the entire ecosystem to the community, including a data processing and training pipeline, a humanoid foundation model, and a real-time action inference engine.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [176] [DIVE: Scaling Diversity in Agentic Task Synthesis for Generalizable Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11076)
*Aili Chen,Chi Zhang,Junteng Liu,Jiangjie Chen,Chengyu Du,Yunji Li,Ming Zhong,Qin Wang,Zhengmao Zhu,Jiayuan Song,Ke Ji,Junxian He,Pengyu Zhao,Yanghua Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DIVE提出了一种证据驱动的任务合成方法，通过先执行多样化真实工具并逆向推导任务，显著提升LLM在工具使用任务上的OOD泛化能力，证明多样性扩展比数据量扩展更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具使用LLM的后训练任务合成方法在任务和工具集变化时泛化能力脆弱，主要原因是合成任务多样性不足。需要解决在保持任务可执行性和可验证性的同时，覆盖多样化工具类型、工具集组合和异构工具使用模式的挑战。

Method: 提出DIVE方法，反转传统合成顺序：1) 先执行多样化的真实世界工具；2) 从执行轨迹中严格逆向推导出任务；3) 通过工具池覆盖和每任务工具集多样性两个可控维度扩展结构多样性；4) 采用证据收集-任务推导循环，在5个领域的373个工具上诱导丰富的多步工具使用模式。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B模型上使用DIVE数据（48k SFT + 3.2k RL）训练，在9个OOD基准测试中平均提升22分，比最强的8B基线高出68分。控制性扩展分析显示，多样性扩展在OOD泛化上始终优于数量扩展，即使数据量少4倍。

Conclusion: DIVE通过证据驱动的任务合成方法，有效解决了工具使用LLM的泛化脆弱性问题，证明了任务多样性扩展比单纯增加数据量对OOD泛化更为关键，为构建更鲁棒的工具使用模型提供了新方向。

Abstract: Recent work synthesizes agentic tasks for post-training tool-using LLMs, yet robust generalization under shifts in tasks and toolsets remains an open challenge. We trace this brittleness to insufficient diversity in synthesized tasks. Scaling diversity is difficult because training requires tasks to remain executable and verifiable, while generalization demands coverage of diverse tool types, toolset combinations, and heterogeneous tool-use patterns. We propose DIVE, an evidence-driven recipe that inverts synthesis order, executing diverse, real-world tools first and reverse-deriving tasks strictly entailed by the resulting traces, thereby providing grounding by construction. DIVE scales structural diversity along two controllable axes, tool-pool coverage and per-task toolset variety, and an Evidence Collection--Task Derivation loop further induces rich multi-step tool-use patterns across 373 tools in five domains. Training Qwen3-8B on DIVE data (48k SFT + 3.2k RL) improves by +22 average points across 9 OOD benchmarks and outperforms the strongest 8B baseline by +68. Remarkably, controlled scaling analysis reveals that diversity scaling consistently outperforms quantity scaling for OOD generalization, even with 4x less data.

</details>


### [177] [A Survey of Reasoning in Autonomous Driving Systems: Open Challenges and Emerging Paradigms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11093)
*Kejin Yu,Yuhan Sun,Taiqiang Wu,Ruixu Zhang,Zhiqiang Lin,Yuxin Meng,Junjie Wang,Yujiu Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出将大型语言和多模态模型作为自动驾驶系统的认知核心，以解决当前系统在长尾场景和复杂社交交互中的推理缺陷，并提出了认知层次框架和七项核心推理挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自动驾驶系统从感知为中心转向推理瓶颈，在长尾场景和复杂社交交互中表现不足，而大型语言和多模态模型为集成强大认知引擎提供了机会，但缺乏系统集成框架。

Method: 提出认知层次框架分解驾驶任务的认知和交互复杂性，系统化七项核心推理挑战，并从系统架构和评估实践两个角度进行综述分析。

Result: 分析揭示了向整体可解释"玻璃盒"智能体发展的趋势，识别了LLM推理的高延迟与车辆控制的毫秒级安全需求之间的根本矛盾。

Conclusion: 未来工作需要通过开发可验证的神经符号架构、不确定性下的鲁棒推理和可扩展的隐式社交协商模型，来弥合符号到物理的鸿沟。

Abstract: The development of high-level autonomous driving (AD) is shifting from perception-centric limitations to a more fundamental bottleneck, namely, a deficit in robust and generalizable reasoning. Although current AD systems manage structured environments, they consistently falter in long-tail scenarios and complex social interactions that require human-like judgment. Meanwhile, the advent of large language and multimodal models (LLMs and MLLMs) presents a transformative opportunity to integrate a powerful cognitive engine into AD systems, moving beyond pattern matching toward genuine comprehension. However, a systematic framework to guide this integration is critically lacking. To bridge this gap, we provide a comprehensive review of this emerging field and argue that reasoning should be elevated from a modular component to the system's cognitive core. Specifically, we first propose a novel Cognitive Hierarchy to decompose the monolithic driving task according to its cognitive and interactive complexity. Building on this, we further derive and systematize seven core reasoning challenges, such as the responsiveness-reasoning trade-off and social-game reasoning. Furthermore, we conduct a dual-perspective review of the state-of-the-art, analyzing both system-centric approaches to architecting intelligent agents and evaluation-centric practices for their validation. Our analysis reveals a clear trend toward holistic and interpretable "glass-box" agents. In conclusion, we identify a fundamental and unresolved tension between the high-latency, deliberative nature of LLM-based reasoning and the millisecond-scale, safety-critical demands of vehicle control. For future work, a primary objective is to bridge the symbolic-to-physical gap by developing verifiable neuro-symbolic architectures, robust reasoning under uncertainty, and scalable models for implicit social negotiation.

</details>


### [178] [PACED: Distillation at the Frontier of Student Competence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11178)
*Yuanda Xu,Hejian Sang,Zhengze Zhou,Ran He,Zhipeng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Paced框架通过聚焦于学生模型的最近发展区（能力边界）来优化LLM蒸馏，使用基于通过率的Beta权重函数w(p)=p^α(1-p)^β，避免在已掌握和过难问题上的计算浪费。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准LLM蒸馏存在计算浪费：在学生已掌握的问题上梯度接近零，在过难问题上梯度不连贯且会损害现有能力。这种浪费在理论上不可避免，因为蒸馏梯度信噪比在通过率极端值处消失。

Method: 提出Paced框架，使用通过率权重函数w(p)=p^α(1-p)^β将蒸馏集中在学生能力的最近发展区。该方法仅需学生rollout估计通过率，无需架构修改，兼容任何KL方向。采用两阶段策略：前向KL（模式覆盖）后接反向KL（巩固）。

Result: 1. 理论证明Beta核是最小极大鲁棒的；2. 从大教师到小学生模型的前向KL蒸馏显著提升性能且保持低遗忘；3. 指令调优模型的反向KL自蒸馏超过基线；4. 两阶段策略（前向KL后接反向KL）在标准推理基准上获得最大改进。

Conclusion: Paced框架通过理论驱动的通过率加权，有效解决了LLM蒸馏中的计算浪费问题，将资源集中在能力边界区域，实现了显著性能提升，支持蒸馏过程的"模式覆盖-巩固"解释。

Abstract: Standard LLM distillation wastes compute on two fronts: problems the student has already mastered (near-zero gradients) and problems far beyond its reach (incoherent gradients that erode existing capabilities). We show that this waste is not merely intuitive but structurally inevitable: the gradient signal-to-noise ratio in distillation provably vanishes at both pass-rate extremes. This theoretical observation leads to Paced, a framework that concentrates distillation on the zone of proximal development -- the frontier of a student model's competence -- via a principled pass-rate weight $w(p) = p^α(1 - p)^β$ derived from the boundary-vanishing structure of distillation gradients. Key results: (1) Theory: We prove that the Beta kernel $w(p) = p^α(1-p)^β$ is a leading-order weight family arising from the SNR structure of distillation, and that it is minimax-robust -- under bounded multiplicative misspecification, worst-case efficiency loss is only $O(δ^2)$. (2)Distillation: On distillation from a larger teacher to a smaller student model with forward KL, Paced achieves significant gain over the base model, while keeping benchmark forgetting at a low level. (3)Self-distillation: On instruction-tuned models with reverse KL, gains are exceeding baselines as well. (4)Two-stage synergy: A forward-KL-then-reverse-KL schedule yields the strongest results in our setting, reaching substantial improvements on standard reasoning benchmarks -- supporting a mode-coverage-then-consolidation interpretation of the distillation process. All configurations require only student rollouts to estimate pass rates, need no architectural changes, and are compatible with any KL direction.

</details>


### [179] [The Unlearning Mirage: A Dynamic Framework for Evaluating LLM Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11266)
*Raj Sanjay Shah,Jing Huang,Keerthiram Murugesan,Nathalie Baracaldo,Diyi Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种动态框架来压力测试大语言模型遗忘方法的鲁棒性，通过复杂结构化查询揭示现有遗忘方法的脆弱性，特别是多跳推理场景下的失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型遗忘方法存在脆弱性：轻微查询修改（如多跳推理和实体别名）就能恢复已遗忘信息。当前评估指标依赖静态非结构化基准，无法检测这些漏洞，造成有效性假象。

Method: 提出动态框架，首先从目标模型（遗忘前）引出知识并构建针对性探针，范围从简单查询到多跳链，精确控制查询难度。通过自动生成语义等效的问答探针进行压力测试。

Result: 框架：(1)通过自动生成语义等效问答探针达到与现有基准相当的覆盖率；(2)与先前评估保持一致；(3)发现其他基准遗漏的新遗忘失败，特别是在多跳设置中。激活分析显示单跳查询通常遵循主导计算路径，更容易被遗忘方法破坏；而多跳查询倾向于使用保持完整的替代路径。

Conclusion: 该框架实现了遗忘方法的实用可扩展评估，无需手动构建遗忘测试集，便于实际应用采用。揭示了遗忘方法在多跳设置中的脆弱性，为更鲁棒的遗忘技术开发提供了评估基础。

Abstract: Unlearning in Large Language Models (LLMs) aims to enhance safety, mitigate biases, and comply with legal mandates, such as the right to be forgotten. However, existing unlearning methods are brittle: minor query modifications, such as multi-hop reasoning and entity aliasing, can recover supposedly forgotten information. As a result, current evaluation metrics often create an illusion of effectiveness, failing to detect these vulnerabilities due to reliance on static, unstructured benchmarks. We propose a dynamic framework that stress tests unlearning robustness using complex structured queries. Our approach first elicits knowledge from the target model (pre-unlearning) and constructs targeted probes, ranging from simple queries to multi-hop chains, allowing precise control over query difficulty. Our experiments show that the framework (1) shows comparable coverage to existing benchmarks by automatically generating semantically equivalent Q&A probes, (2) aligns with prior evaluations, and (3) uncovers new unlearning failures missed by other benchmarks, particularly in multi-hop settings. Furthermore, activation analyses show that single-hop queries typically follow dominant computation pathways, which are more likely to be disrupted by unlearning methods. In contrast, multi-hop queries tend to use alternative pathways that often remain intact, explaining the brittleness of unlearning techniques in multi-hop settings. Our framework enables practical and scalable evaluation of unlearning methods without the need for manual construction of forget test sets, enabling easier adoption for real-world applications. We release the pip package and the code at https://sites.google.com/view/unlearningmirage/home.

</details>


### [180] [COMPASS: The explainable agentic framework for Sovereignty, Sustainability, Compliance, and Ethics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11277)
*Jean-Sébastien,Dessureault,Alain-Thierry,Iliho Manzi,Soukaina,Alaoui Ismaili,Khadim,Lo,Mireille,Lalancette,Éric,Bélanger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: COMPASS框架是一个多智能体编排系统，通过模块化治理机制强制执行价值对齐的AI，整合主权、碳感知计算、合规性和伦理四个维度，使用RAG增强评估并采用LLM-as-a-judge方法进行实时仲裁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型智能体系统的快速扩散引发了数字主权、环境可持续性、监管合规和伦理对齐等关键问题。现有框架仅孤立地处理单个维度，缺乏统一架构将这些要求系统性地整合到自主智能体的决策过程中。

Method: 提出COMPASS框架，包含一个编排器和四个专门子智能体（主权、碳感知计算、合规性、伦理），每个子智能体都通过检索增强生成(RAG)技术增强，基于已验证的上下文特定文档进行评估。采用LLM-as-a-judge方法为每个评估维度分配量化分数并生成可解释的论证，实现冲突目标的实时仲裁。

Result: 通过自动化评估验证了该架构，证明RAG集成显著增强了语义一致性并减轻了幻觉风险。框架的基于组合的设计便于无缝集成到不同应用领域，同时保持可解释性和可追溯性。

Conclusion: COMPASS框架为多维度原则在自主系统中的系统集成提供了统一架构，通过模块化治理机制实现价值对齐AI，解决了现有方法在整合数字主权、可持续性、合规性和伦理方面的不足。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of large language model (LLM)-based agentic systems raises critical concerns regarding digital sovereignty, environmental sustainability, regulatory compliance, and ethical alignment. Whilst existing frameworks address individual dimensions in isolation, no unified architecture systematically integrates these imperatives into the decision-making processes of autonomous agents. This paper introduces the COMPASS (Compliance and Orchestration for Multi-dimensional Principles in Autonomous Systems with Sovereignty) Framework, a novel multi-agent orchestration system designed to enforce value-aligned AI through modular, extensible governance mechanisms. The framework comprises an Orchestrator and four specialised sub-agents addressing sovereignty, carbon-aware computing, compliance, and ethics, each augmented with Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) to ground evaluations in verified, context-specific documents. By employing an LLM-as-a-judge methodology, the system assigns quantitative scores and generates explainable justifications for each assessment dimension, enabling real-time arbitration of conflicting objectives. We validate the architecture through automated evaluation, demonstrating that RAG integration significantly enhances semantic coherence and mitigates the hallucination risks. Our results indicate that the framework's composition-based design facilitates seamless integration into diverse application domains whilst preserving interpretability and traceability.

</details>


### [181] [AI Psychometrics: Evaluating the Psychological Reasoning of Large Language Models with Psychometric Validities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11279)
*Yibai Li,Xiaolin Lin,Zhenghui Sha,Zhiye Jin,Xiaobing Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究应用AI心理测量学方法评估GPT-3.5、GPT-4、LLaMA-2和LLaMA-3四个大型语言模型的心理推理能力和心理测量效度，发现所有模型均满足效度标准，且性能更高的模型表现出更优的心理测量效度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型参数庞大、结构复杂，如同"黑箱"系统难以评估和解释。AI心理测量学作为新兴领域，旨在应用心理测量学方法来评估和解释人工智能系统的心理特征和过程。

Method: 采用技术接受模型（TAM）框架，对GPT-3.5、GPT-4、LLaMA-2和LLaMA-3四个模型进行收敛效度、区分效度、预测效度和外部效度的综合评估。

Result: 所有模型的响应均满足各项效度标准。性能更高的模型（GPT-4和LLaMA-3）相比其前代模型（GPT-3.5和LLaMA-2）表现出更优的心理测量效度。

Conclusion: 研究结果证实了应用AI心理测量学评估和解释大型语言模型的有效性，为理解这些复杂系统的心理特征提供了方法论基础。

Abstract: The immense number of parameters and deep neural networks make large language models (LLMs) rival the complexity of human brains, which also makes them opaque ``black box'' systems that are challenging to evaluate and interpret. AI Psychometrics is an emerging field that aims to tackle these challenges by applying psychometric methodologies to evaluate and interpret the psychological traits and processes of artificial intelligence (AI) systems. This paper investigates the application of AI Psychometrics to evaluate the psychological reasoning and overall psychometric validity of four prominent LLMs: GPT-3.5, GPT-4, LLaMA-2, and LLaMA-3. Using the Technology Acceptance Model (TAM), we examined convergent, discriminant, predictive, and external validity across these models. Our findings reveal that the responses from all these models generally met all validity criteria. Moreover, higher-performing models like GPT-4 and LLaMA-3 consistently demonstrated superior psychometric validity compared to their predecessors, GPT-3.5 and LLaMA-2. These results help to establish the validity of applying AI Psychometrics to evaluate and interpret large language models.

</details>


### [182] [Counterweights and Complementarities: The Convergence of AI and Blockchain Powering a Decentralized Future](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11299)
*Yibai Li,Zhiye Jin,Xiaobing,Li,K. D. Joshi,Xuefei,Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AI与区块链技术融合：AI趋向中心化而区块链提供去中心化解决方案，两者互补形成"去中心化智能"新研究领域


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨人工智能与区块链技术之间的关键交叉点，解决AI（特别是大语言模型）因数据和资源垄断而产生的中心化趋势问题，同时利用区块链的去中心化特性来平衡这种趋势

Method: 通过理论分析和概念框架构建，提出区块链可以通过去中心化数据管理、计算和治理来缓解AI的中心化风险，而AI则能通过自动化智能合约管理、内容策展和威胁检测来增强区块链的效率和安全性

Result: 论证了AI和区块链技术具有互补优势，提出了"去中心化智能"这一跨学科研究领域的概念框架，旨在创建无需集中控制的智能系统

Conclusion: AI与区块链技术不是相互排斥的，而是具有互补优势。区块链可以缓解AI的中心化风险，促进包容性、透明度和用户隐私；AI可以增强区块链的效率和安全性。需要发展"去中心化智能"这一新兴研究领域

Abstract: This editorial addresses the critical intersection of artificial intelligence (AI) and blockchain technologies, highlighting their contrasting tendencies toward centralization and decentralization, respectively. While AI, particularly with the rise of large language models (LLMs), exhibits a strong centralizing force due to data and resource monopolization by large corporations, blockchain offers a counterbalancing mechanism through its inherent decentralization, transparency, and security. The editorial argues that these technologies are not mutually exclusive but possess complementary strengths. Blockchain can mitigate AI's centralizing risks by enabling decentralized data management, computation, and governance, promoting greater inclusivity, transparency, and user privacy. Conversely, AI can enhance blockchain's efficiency and security through automated smart contract management, content curation, and threat detection. The core argument calls for the development of ``decentralized intelligence'' (DI) -- an interdisciplinary research area focused on creating intelligent systems that function without centralized control.

</details>


### [183] [FinRule-Bench: A Benchmark for Joint Reasoning over Financial Tables and Principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11339)
*Arun Vignesh Malarkkan,Manan Roy Choudhury,Guangwei Zhang,Vivek Gupta,Qingyun Wang,Yanjie Fu,Denghui Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FinRule-Bench是一个评估大语言模型在真实财务报表上进行基于规则的财务推理诊断完整性的基准测试，包含三种渐进式审计任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型在财务分析中的应用日益增多，但它们在明确会计准则下审计结构化财务报表的能力尚未得到充分探索。现有基准主要评估问答、数值推理或在合成数据上的异常检测，无法确定模型能否可靠地验证或定位正确财务报表的规则合规性。

Method: 引入FinRule-Bench基准，包含真实财务报表与人工策划的明确会计准则配对，涵盖四种标准报表类型。定义三种渐进式审计任务：规则验证、规则识别和联合规则诊断。采用零样本和少样本提示评估LLMs，并引入因果反事实推理协议来强制决策、解释和反事实判断之间的一致性。

Result: 研究发现，虽然模型在孤立规则验证上表现良好，但在规则区分和多违规诊断任务上性能急剧下降。FinRule-Bench为研究LLMs在高风险财务分析中的规则治理推理、诊断覆盖范围和失败模式提供了原则性和可复现的测试平台。

Conclusion: FinRule-Bench填补了现有基准的空白，为评估LLMs在基于规则的财务审计中的诊断完整性提供了系统框架。结果表明，当前LLMs在复杂规则推理和多违规诊断方面仍有显著局限，需要进一步研究提升模型在财务合规性检查中的可靠性和覆盖范围。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly applied to financial analysis, yet their ability to audit structured financial statements under explicit accounting principles remains poorly explored. Existing benchmarks primarily evaluate question answering, numerical reasoning, or anomaly detection on synthetically corrupted data, making it unclear whether models can reliably verify or localize rule compliance on correct financial statements. We introduce FinRule-Bench, a benchmark for evaluating diagnostic completeness in rule-based financial reasoning over real-world financial tables. FinRule-Bench pairs ground-truth financial statements with explicit, human-curated accounting principles and spans four canonical statement types: Balance Sheets, Cash Flow Statements, Income Statements, and Statements of Equity. The benchmark defines three auditing tasks that require progressively stronger reasoning capabilities: (i) rule verification, which tests compliance with a single principle; (ii) rule identification, which requires selecting the violated principle from a provided rule set; and (iii) joint rule diagnosis, which requires detecting and localizing multiple simultaneous violations at the record level. We evaluate LLMs under zero-shot and few-shot prompting, and introduce a causal-counterfactual reasoning protocol that enforces consistency between decisions, explanations, and counterfactual judgments. Across tasks and statement types, we find that while models perform well on isolated rule verification, performance degrades sharply for rule discrimination and multi-violation diagnosis. FinRule-Bench provides a principled and reproducible testbed for studying rule-governed reasoning, diagnostic coverage, and failure modes of LLMs in high-stakes financial analysis.

</details>


### [184] [Improving LLM Performance Through Black-Box Online Tuning: A Case for Adding System Specs to Factsheets for Trusted AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11340)
*Yonas Atinafu,Henry Lin,Robin Cohen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于端到端测量的黑盒在线控制器，通过爬山算法最大化满足SLO的请求吞吐量，并以LLM服务为例探讨AI系统事实表中集成性能与可持续性指标的重要性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统控制器需要内部仪器化，而黑盒控制器仅使用端到端测量，无需内部访问，旨在最大化满足服务级别目标(SLO)的请求吞吐量(良好吞吐量)

Method: 设计基于端到端测量的黑盒在线控制器，仅使用短时间段内的测量数据，通过爬山算法优化系统参数以最大化良好吞吐量

Result: 提供了经验证据表明该设计是合理的，并以LLM服务作为具体案例展示了该方法的有效性

Conclusion: 该控制器设计为组织采用AI系统时，在事实表中集成系统性能和可持续性指标提供了重要参考，强调了端到端测量方法在优化AI系统服务中的价值

Abstract: In this paper, we present a novel black-box online controller that uses only end-to-end measurements over short segments, without internal instrumentation, and hill climbing to maximize goodput, defined as the throughput of requests that satisfy the service-level objective. We provide empirical evidence that this design is well-founded. Using this advance in LLM serving as a concrete example, we then discuss the importance of integrating system performance and sustainability metrics into Factsheets for organizations adopting AI systems.

</details>


### [185] [TimeSqueeze: Dynamic Patching for Efficient Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11352)
*Sravan Kumar Ankireddy,Nikita Seleznev,Nam H. Nguyen,Yulun Wu,Senthil Kumar,Furong Huang,C. Bayan Bruss*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TimeSqueeze是一种动态分块机制，通过基于局部信号复杂度自适应选择分块边界，解决了Transformer时间序列基础模型中点级嵌入与固定长度分块之间的权衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer时间序列基础模型面临tokenization的基本权衡：点级嵌入保持时间保真度但序列长度扩展性差，固定长度分块提高效率但可能破坏自然过渡并模糊信息丰富的局部动态。

Method: TimeSqueeze首先应用轻量级状态空间编码器提取全分辨率点级特征，然后基于内容感知的分割，将短分块分配给信息密集区域，长分块分配给平滑或冗余段，实现可变分辨率压缩。

Result: 在大规模预训练中，TimeSqueeze相比等效点级token基准实现了高达20倍的收敛加速和8倍的数据效率提升。在长期预测基准测试中，TimeSqueeze始终优于使用点级tokenization或固定大小分块的类似架构。

Conclusion: TimeSqueeze通过动态自适应分块机制，在保持关键时间结构的同时显著减少Transformer主干处理的token序列，有效解决了时间序列建模中tokenization的效率和保真度权衡问题。

Abstract: Transformer-based time series foundation models face a fundamental trade-off in choice of tokenization: point-wise embeddings preserve temporal fidelity but scale poorly with sequence length, whereas fixed-length patching improves efficiency by imposing uniform boundaries that may disrupt natural transitions and blur informative local dynamics. In order to address these limitations, we introduce TimeSqueeze, a dynamic patching mechanism that adaptively selects patch boundaries within each sequence based on local signal complexity. TimeSqueeze first applies a lightweight state-space encoder to extract full-resolution point-wise features, then performs content-aware segmentation by allocating short patches to information-dense regions and long patches to smooth or redundant segments. This variable-resolution compression preserves critical temporal structure while substantially reducing the token sequence presented to the Transformer backbone.
